Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the rings framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 681. The resulting operator achieves bound O(259/n^4).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on rings structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 681. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 259. QED.
